                  (↑H).Pairwise Disjoint →

                      IntegrationInACVF.Section3.MinimalBallSupport.IsMinimalSupport balls G

                        ∀ {U : J → Set X},

                                        IntegrationInACVF.Section3.PuncturedDecomposition.region G (U j) ⊆
                                          P \ {x | ∃ h ∈ H, x ∈ h}} =
                                  {i}

@IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.OrderedFiniteCellCount.cardinal : ∀ {α : Type u_1} {ι : Type u_2} [inst : LinearOrder α]
  [DenselyOrdered α] (D P : Set α),
  D.OrdConnected →
    P.Finite →
      ∀ (C : ι → Set α) (r : ι → α),
        (∀ (x : α), x ∈ D ↔ x ∈ P ∨ ∃ i, x ∈ C i) →
          (∀ (i : ι), Disjoint P (C i)) →
            (∀ (i j : ι), i ≠ j → Disjoint (C i) (C j)) →
              (∀ (i : ι), (C i).Nonempty) →
                (∀ (i : ι), (C i).OrdConnected) →
                  (∀ (i : ι), ∀ x ∈ C i, ∃ y ∈ C i, y < x) →
                    (∀ (i : ι), ∀ x ∈ C i, ∃ y ∈ C i, x < y) →
                      (∀ (i : ι), IsLUB (C i) (r i)) →
                        (∀ (i : ι), r i ∈ D) → Nat.card ι = {p | p ∈ P ∧ ∃ x ∈ D, x < p}.ncard

      D.OrdConnected →
        ∀ (b : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma),
          IsGreatest D b →
            ∀ (P : Finset SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma)
              (C : ι → Set SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma),
              (∀ (x : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma), x ∈ D ↔ x ∈ P ∨ ∃ i, x ∈ C i) →

                  (∀ (i j : ι), i ≠ j → Disjoint (C i) (C j)) →
                    (∀ (i : ι), (C i).Nonempty) →
                      (∀ (i : ι), (C i).OrdConnected) →
                        (∀ (i : ι), ∀ x ∈ C i, ∃ y ∈ C i, y < x) →

                            (∀ (i : ι), ∃ B, IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.FullGammaSyntax.Scalar B (C i)) →
                              ∃ K, K.points = P ∧ K.cells = C

      (f : M → M),
      IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.FullBaseAtomicity.OverBase B
          (IntegrationInACVF.Section3.ImaginaryBaseImages.graphOn
            (IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true) f) →
        ∀
          (hfVF :
            ∀ x ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true,
              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF (f x))
          (c : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
          ↑c ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true →
            ∃ r,
              IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.LinearRadiusThreshold.IsLeastLinearRadius
                (IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true) f hfVF c r
@IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.LeastLinearRadiusExistence.ordinary_function : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF],
  M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
    ∀ (B : IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.FullBaseAtomicity.Base)
      (a : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType) (α : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma)
      (f : M → M),
      IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.FullBaseAtomicity.OverBase B
          (IntegrationInACVF.Section3.ImaginaryBaseImages.graphOn

        ∀
          (hfVF :

              SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF (f x)),
          ∃ threshold,
            ∀ (c : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),

                IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.LinearRadiusThreshold.IsLeastLinearRadius
                  (IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true) f hfVF c (threshold c)
@IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.LeastLinearRadiusExistence.corollary_3_17_ordinary : ∀ {M : Type}
  [sM : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.LRV.Structure M] [hM : M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF]
  {κ : Cardinal.{0}},
  Cardinal.aleph0 ≤ κ →
    SpecialTransformations.Section3.Saturated M κ →
      ∀ (B : IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.FullBaseAtomicity.Base),
        Cardinal.mk ↑B.real < κ →
          M ⊨ SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Axioms.ACVF00 →
            ∀ (a : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType) (α : SpecialTransformations.Section3.Values.Gamma)
              (f : M → M),
              IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.FullBaseAtomicity.OverBase B
                  (IntegrationInACVF.Section3.ImaginaryBaseImages.graphOn
                    (IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true) f) →
                ∀
                  (hfVF :
                    ∀ x ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true,
                      SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.iVF (f x)),
                  Set.InjOn f (IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true) →
                    ∃ threshold,
                      (∀ (c : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
                          ↑c ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true →
                            IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.LinearRadiusThreshold.IsLeastLinearRadius
                              (IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true) f hfVF c
                              (threshold c)) ∧
                        ¬∃ β,
                            α < β ∧
                              ∀ (c : SpecialTransformations.Milestone0.Transfer.VFType),
                                ↑c ∈ IntegrationInACVF.Section3ACVF.GammaRadiusAtomicity.gammaBall a α Bool.true →
                                  ↑β < threshold c
